# PTE Write From Dictation - Segment Practice

**Total Segments**: 3059
**Source Sentences**: 1195
**Format**: Individual thought group segments for focused pronunciation practice
**Purpose**: Practice each segment separately to improve natural pausing and intonation
**Last Updated**: December 2024

---

## About This Practice Method

This dataset breaks down complete sentences into **thought groups** (sense groups) - natural speech segments where native speakers pause briefly.

**How to Practice**:
- Listen to each segment individually
- Repeat immediately after hearing it
- Focus on natural rhythm and intonation
- Move to the next segment
- Practice complete sentences by combining segments

**Benefits**:
- Master natural pausing points
- Improve speech rhythm and flow
- Build muscle memory for complex sentences
- Sound more natural and fluent

---
